For each correlation line (linear), change = a_j*m*delta + a_p*m*(-delta) = (a_j - a_p)*(x-old_j). So net coefficient A. For quadratic lag0: term for j: x(1-x)-old_j(1-old_j); term for p: p(1-p)-old_p(1-old_p). Both are quadratic in x (p linear). The sum is concave quadratic? q(x) = x - x^2 + p - p^2; p = α - x. But if a correlation contains product h_j*h_p (when h_p and h_j are paired at some lag), the function is quadratic and derivative coefficients depend on each other in nonlinear way. Our linear coefficient a_j includes terms from pairs involving j with neighbors (including p if offset p-j), and a_p includes pair with j. If both change, the product term changes not simply a_j delta_j + a_p delta_p? Let's check product term h_j(1-h_p) = h_j - h_j h_p. At old, derivative w.r.t h_j: 1-h_p_old; w.r.t h_p: -h_j_old. Let's design projected gradient with smooth max and topk.
Objective: We want minimize f = max(c). Use smooth approximation: F(h)= (1/β) log sum exp(β(c - M)) + M, where M=max c. Scale dx constant ignored. Gradient: w_l = exp(β(c_l-M))/sum; grad = ∑ w_l ∂c_l/∂h. Since c max ≤~80, β maybe 50-5000. c values close, logsumexp stable.
We can compute gradient efficiently. For each nonzero weight w_d, we can update grad as coefficients:
For lag d=0: grad[j] += w0*(1-2h_j) for each j? Actually derivative of c0 wrt h_j = 1-2h_j. Weighted sum w0 added to each grad[j].
For lag d≠0: c_d = ∑_{i: i+d in range} h_i(1-h_{i+d}). Derivative:
grad[i] += 1 - h_{i+d}; grad[i+d] += -h_i.
Thus for weight w, grad[0:N-d] += w*(1-h[d:N]) if d>0? For d<0 adjust.
We can loop over nonzero weights (all d maybe) and vector add. For N=160, 160 d, vector length N, per iteration 160 loops, okay. For N=512, 512 loops *512 ~262k ops.
Projection onto box+sum:
Given y = h - lr*g. Need x=clip(y + θ) sum target. θ real. sum(clip(y+θ),0,1) monotone in θ. Bisection 30 iterations. O(N) each; per iteration maybe okay.
Stepsize: Use backtracking Armijo on smooth F. We need F evaluation O(N^2?) via correlate O(N^2) or maintain c? For gradient iteration, can compute c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full') each iteration O(N^2) (C fast). For N=160, 25k, 10000 iterations =>250M, maybe ok? 1300s maybe. Need optimize. We can compute F and c; gradient loop uses c. 10000*25k=250M C operations + Python 1.6M vector ops, maybe borderline but ok (numpy correlate in C 25k floats ~50us? 10000=0.5s? Actually 25k mult 10k=250M ~0.1s per 100M? maybe 2s). Good.

Let's derive formula for contribution of pair (a,b) offset d:
Term T = h_a (1 - h_b). Here a is first index, b=a+d. Under update u->x, v=s-x (v new). Other fixed. Cases:
1. a=u, b=v: T = x (1 - (s-x)) = x(1-s+x) = x^2 + (1-s)x.
2. a=v, b=u: T = (s-x)(1-x) = s - (s+1)x + x^2.
3. a=u, b=o (fixed b): T = x(1 - h_b) linear: a_coeff=(1-h_b).
4. a=o, b=u: T = h_o(1-x) = h_o - h_o x.
5. a=v, b=o: T = (s-x)(1-h_b) = s(1-h_b) - (1-h_b)x (linear coeff -(1-h_b)).
6. a=o, b=v: T = h_o(1-(s-x)) = h_o(1-s) + h_o x (linear coeff h_o).
7. d=0 pair a=b=u: T=x(1-x); a=b=v: (s-x)(1 - (s-x)). These quadratics.

def feasible_at(t, lo, hi, Umax, A2,A1,A0):
    Ls=[lo]; Us=[hi]
    if t < Umax - 1e-12: return False
    for idx in range(len(A2)):
       a=A2[idx]; b=A1[idx]; c=A0[idx]
       if abs(a)<1e-14:
          if abs(b)<1e-14:
             if c - t > 1e-11: return False
             continue
          if b>0:
             ub=(t-c)/b
             newL=[];newU=[]
             for L,U in zip(Ls,Us):
                if U > ub: U=ub
                if L <= U + tol: append
          else similar
       else:
          # compute roots
       if no intervals return False
    return True

Pseudo:
def intersect_quad(Ls, Us, a, b, c, t):
    c = c - t
    # p = a x^2 + b x + c <=0
    if a > 1e-14:
       disc = b*b - 4*a*c
       if disc < -1e-12: return []
       if disc < 0: disc=0
       sqrt=np.sqrt(disc)
       r1 = (-b - sqrt)/(2*a); r2 = (-b + sqrt)/(2*a)
       if r1>r2 swap (though a>0 r1<r2)
       segs=[(r1,r2)]
    elif a < -1e-14:
       disc = b*b - 4*a*c  # a neg
       if disc < -1e-12:
          # p always? a<0 and no roots -> p<0 all? Let's verify if disc<0, polynomial sign = a <0 for all, so <=0 all.
          return Ls,Us unchanged
       if disc <= 1e-12:
          # all feasible (or root point)
          return unchanged
       sqrt=np.sqrt(disc)
       roots = [(-b - sqrt)/(2*a), (-b + sqrt)/(2*a)]; sort
       segs=[(Ls? global -inf, roots[0]), (roots[1], inf)] but we'll intersect with each current interval.
    else:
       if abs(b)<eps: if c>tol return []; else unchanged
       else line as above.
    newL=[];newU=[]
    for segL,segU in segs:
      for L,U in zip(Ls,Us):
         l=max(L,segL); u=min(U,segU)
         if l <= u+ttol: append
    return newL,newU

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-5
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_h=None; best_c5=inf; best_n=0
    # load parent
    if initial_h_values available: n_parent=len; h=project_feasible(initial_h_values,n); c5=eval
    else: construct default n=192 maybe low? We can use random? But parent should available. Still implement.
    # ensure n even? If odd maybe add one via interpolation? Use as is.
    # Phase A: n_parent pair coordinate
    if time left >300: h2=pair_coord_descent(h,n_parent,sweeps=?,deadline=deadline-200)
    update best.
    # Phase B: local smooth projected gradient on best? optional after coordinate maybe to escape?
    # Phase C: perturbations at same n:
    t = current
    perturb_styles: random gaussian scale, random binary threshold of h+noise, random pair flips (while preserving sum), rotations? Shift? h shift not allowed?        scale 0.01,0.05,0.1
       maybe binary: b = (best_h + noise) > 0.5, adjust sum, project? For binary with fractional? Use b float 0/1.

def build_pair_data(h,c,u,v,masks, first_m, first_b, second_m, second_a):
    n=len(h); M=2*n-1; center=n-1
    ou=h[u]; ov=h[v]; s=ou+ov; lo=max(0,s-1); hi=min(1,s)
    mask = masks[u] | masks[v]
    idx = np.nonzero(mask)[0]

    for m_arr, const_a, b_arr, const_b? Actually for first: a=u, b varies.

          seen.add(code)
          add_contrib(m,u,b,...)
    # u second: a varies, b=u
       for pos,m in enumerate(second_m[u]):
          a=second_a[u][pos]
          code=a*M+u; ... add
    # v first, v second similarly
    # after, restrict to idx? We can use full arrays and idx.

Let's design vectorized build:
- We will process linear occurrences where exactly one of a,b is the updated variable and the other is fixed (not u/v). Cases:
  For variable u as a (first), with b not in {u,v} and d !=0 (b != u): m in first_m[u] where b != u and b != v.
  For variable u as b (second), with a not in {u,v} and d !=0: m in second_m[u] where a != v and a != u.
  Similarly for v with x replaced by v = s-x; but since we are variable x for u, v new = s-x. Linear occurrences for v (exactly one variable v, other fixed) have coefficients in x (not y) as derived: if a=v,b=f: (s-x)(1-h_f)=s(1-h_f) - (1-h_f)x. If a=f,b=v: h_f(1-(s-x))=h_f(1-s)+h_f x.
- Self quadratic: add for u at center: -x^2 + x; for v: (s-x)(1-(s-x)) = -x^2 + (2s-1)x + (s-s^2). Both at same m=center, combine.
- Cross quadratic u-v: pair (u,v) if offset d=v-u (could positive/negative). In c_m, there is exactly one pair if 0≤d<N and 0≤u+d<N, etc. Actually if d=v-u, both indices in range, pair (u,v) with a=u,b=v if d>0? If v>u, d>0. If v<u, then pair (v,u) with d=u-v >0? Since pair first index a < b. Offset d positive if b>a. We need identify a=min(u,v), b=max(u,v), m=center + (b-a). Term orientation: if a,b with a<b, offset d=b-a >0, term h_a(1-h_b). If u<v: a=u,b=v => term x(1-(s-x)) => x^2+(1-s)x. If v<u: a=v,b=u => term (s-x)(1-x) => x^2 - (s+1)x + s. Add to m_cross.
Also, are there linear occurrences involving the other variable in same m? For cross m, u first with b=v is excluded; v second with a=u excluded. Other occurrences of u/v in same m: u could also pair with another index in same offset (since correlation sum includes multiple pairs at same d). For example u appears as first with b=v, and also as second with a' (not v) in same m? For offset d=b-a, u can be second if u-d = u-(v-u)=2u-v within range and !=v. That occurrence is linear and included in second_m[u] (a not v). Similarly v can first with b'. So vectorized linear includes all except the cross pair. Good.

def project_feasible(h,n):
   h=np.array(h,dtype=float).copy()
   h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   target=n/2.0
   for _ in range(50):
      s=np.sum(h)
      diff=target-s
      if abs(diff)<=1e-15: break
      if diff>0:
         # add to indices with room; not all if enough room
         room=1-h
         # distribute diff to largest rooms? simple uniform among room>0
         mask=room>1e-15
         if not mask.any(): break
         # if diff <= sum room? yes if feasible; distribute uniformly maybe some exceed if diff large
         add=diff/np.sum(mask)
         # To avoid exceed, use waterfilling: sort rooms descending add min(room, remaining)
         # Simpler loop: while diff>1e-14:
      ...
```
Need robust for large initial (random). Implement water fill:
```
def add_to_sum(h, target):
   target=n/2
   for it in range(1000):
      diff=target-sum
      if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
      if diff>0:
         idx=np.argsort(1-h)[::-1]
         rem=diff
         for i in idx:
            room=1-h[i]
            if rem<=1e-16: break
            add=min(room, rem)
            if add>0: h[i]+=add; rem-=add
      else:
         idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
         rem=-diff
         for i in idx:
            rem_i=h[i]
            add=min(rem_i, rem)
            if add>0: h[i]-=add; rem-=add
   # final exact adjust
   diff=target-sum
   if diff!=0:
      if diff>0: idx=int(np.argmax(1-h)); add=min(diff,1-h[idx]); h[idx]+=add
      else: idx=int(np.argmin(h)); add=min(-diff,h[idx]); h[idx]-=add
   return clip
```
This is O(n log n + n*loops). Called many times; initial only. For perturb small, simple average enough. We can have `quick_project(h,n)` for small perturb:

h=clip; diff=target-sum; h+=diff/n; h=clip; repeat few times adjusting calves.
```

 M=2*n-1; center=n-1

    # masks: m_first and m_second bool
    mask=np.zeros(M,bool); mask[m_first]=True; mask[m_second]=True
    store
 return dict

Now, `pair_coord_descent`:
```
def pair_coord_descent(h, n, max_sweeps, deadline, rng, min_time=20):
    struct=build_structures(n)
    h=quick_project(h,n)
    c=correlate(h)
    best=c.max(); best_h=h.copy(); best_val=best*2/n
    sweeps_done=0
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
       if time.time()>deadline-min_time: break
       # choose deterministic/random pairs
       # We can alternate pair patterns:
       # pattern 0: adjacent pairs (0,1), (2,3) maybe n/2? Or every u with u+1 (n updates)
       # To update all variables, use u from 0..n-1, v=(u+1)%n. This includes duplicates but ok.
       # pattern 1: opposite v=(u + n//2)%n
       # pattern 2: random
       for u in range(n):
          if time.time()>deadline-min_time: break
          if sweep % 3 ==0: v=(u+1)%n
          elif sweep %3==1: v=(u + max(1,n//2))%n
          else:
             v=rng.randint(n)
             if v==u: v=(u+1)%n
          h,c,improved=pair_update(...)
       # after sweep, recompute c to correct and project h
       c=correlate(h)
       h=quick_project(h,n)
       if c.max()*2/n < best_val: save
       sweeps_done++
    return best_h,best_val
```
But calling pair_update for u,v and then v,u later effectively same line? For pair (u,v), both orderings produce same feasible line (x vs swap). If order u,v, we vary u; later v,u vary v. Together they cover line with reversing variable, should be equivalent? If we update v alone with u pivot, it's same as updating u but x for v. It might find a different point if previous u not at optimum? Actually the line optimum is independent of which variable parameterized. Updating (u,v) with u x finds global minimizer on line; so updating (v,u) later from that point will find same (or equivalent) point. Duplicates waste. We can instead iterate over unordered pairs to cover variable pairs:
- For pattern adjacent: for i in range(0,n,2): update (i,i+1) maybe last (n-1,0) if n odd? But this updates n/2 pairs; each variable in one pair. ```
def make_perturbed(h,n,rng,scale):
   p = h + scale*rng.randn(n); return project_feasible(p,n)
def make_binary_perturbed(h,n,rng,scale=0.05):
   p=h+scale*rng.randn(n)
   b=(p>=0.5).astype(float)
   # adjust sum by sorting p: choose n/2 largest p as ones maybe
   target=n//2
   if b.sum()>target:
      idx=np.where(b==1)[0]
      order=idx[np.argsort(p[idx])] # remove lowest among ones
      excess=int(b.sum()-target)
      b[order[:excess]]=0
   elif b.sum()<target:
      idx=np.where(b==0)[0]
      order=idx[np.argsort(-p[idx])]
      deficit=target-int(b.sum())
      b[order[:deficit]]=1
   return b
```

`compute_c5(h)`:
```
def compute_c5(h):
    n=len(h)
    if n==0: return inf
    dx=2.0/n
    corr=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr))*dx
```
`correlate_array(h)` returns unscaled corr.

`project_feasible(h,n)`: Robust.
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.array(h,dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size != n: h=h[:n] ...
    np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
    target=float(n)/2.0
    for _ in range(100):
       diff=target - float(np.sum(h))
       if abs(diff) < 1e-15: break
       if diff > 0:
          mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-15
          if not np.any(mask): break
          # water fill to avoid exceeding
          rem=diff
          idx=np.argsort(1.0-h)[::-1]
          for ii in idx:
             room=1.0-h[ii]
             if room <= 1e-15: continue
             add=room if room < rem else rem
             h[ii]+=add
             rem-=add
             if rem <= 1e-15: break
       else:
          mask = h > 1e-15
          if not np.any(mask): break
          rem=-diff
          idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
          for ii in idx:
             avail=h[ii]
             if avail <=1e-15: continue
             add=avail if avail<rem else rem
             h[ii]-=add
             rem-=add
             if rem <=1e-15: break
    np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
    diff=target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
       if diff >0:
          ii=int(np.argmax(1.0-h)); room=1-h[ii]; add=min(diff,room); h[ii]+=add
       else:
          ii=int(np.argmin(h)); avail=h[ii]; add=min(-diff,avail); h[ii]-=add
    return h
```
This water fill loops over all idx for big diff; ok.
`quick_project(h,n)`:
```
def quick_project(h,n):
   h=np.array(h,float).copy(); np.clip(0,1,out=h); target=n/2
   for _ in range(20):
      diff=target - sum
      if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
      if diff>0:
         mask=h<1-1e-14
         if any: h[mask]+=diff/count
      else:
         mask=h>1e-14
         if any: h[mask]+=diff/count
      np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
   # final adjust as above
```

`build_structures(n)` returns tuple:

def build_structures(n):
    M=2*n-1; center=n-1
    first_m=[None]*n; first_b=[None]*n; second_m=[None]*n; second_a=[None]*n; masks=[None]*n
    for i in range(n):
       d_first=np.arange(-i, n-i, dtype=np.int64) # length n
       m_first=center+d_first
       b_first=i+d_first
       d_second=np.arange(i-n+1, i+1, dtype=np.int64)
       m_second=center+d_second
       a_second=i-d_second
       mask=np.zeros(M,dtype=bool)
       mask[m_first]=True
       mask[m_second]=True
       first_m[i]=m_first.astype(np.int64) # maybe m_first already int
       ...
    return first_m, first_b, second_m, second_a, masks
```
Check lengths: `np.arange(-i, n-i)` endpoint exclusive; for i=0: arange(0,n) length n (d=0..n-1). Wait earlier first d∈[-i,N-1-i]. For i=0: d∈[0,N-1], m center..2center. Good. For i=N-1: arange(-(N-1),1) length N: d=-N+1..0. Good. `b_first=i+d`: for i=0, 0..N-1; for i=N-1, 0..N-1. Good.
Second: d∈[i-N+1,i]. `np.arange(i-n+1, i+1)` length N. For i=0: -N+1..0; for i=N-1:0..N-1. `a_second=i-d`: for i=0: 0..N-1 reversed? d=-N+1 -> a=N-1, d=0->0. Good.

`intersect_quad_intervals(Ls, Us, a, b, c0, t, tol)` top-level.
Return tuple (Ls,Us) or None. Need avoid modifying input? It can create new lists.
Implementation:
```
def intersect_quad_intervals(Ls, Us, a, b, c0, t, tol=1e-12):
    c = c0 - t
    # We are on intervals within [lo,hi] already.
    if abs(a) <= 1e-14:
       if abs(b) <= 1e-14:
          if c > tol: return None
          return (Ls, Us)
       # linear
       newL=[]; newU=[]
       if b > 0:
          ub = (t - c0) / b # actually b x + c0 <= t => x <= (t-c0)/b
          for L,U in zip(Ls,Us):
             if U > ub: U=ub
             if L <= U + tol: newL.append(L); newU.append(U)
       else:
          lb = (t - c0) / b
          for L,U in zip:
             if L < lb: L=lb
             if L <= U+tol: append
       if not newL: return None
       return (newL,newU)
    disc = b*b - 4.0*a*c
    if a > 0:
       if disc < -tol: return None
       if disc < 0: disc=0.0
       sqrt=np.sqrt(disc)
       r1=(-b - sqrt)/(2*a); r2=(-b + sqrt)/(2*a)
       if r1 > r2: swap
       segs=[(r1,r2)]
    else:
       # a <0
       if disc < -tol:
          return (Ls,Us)
       if disc <= tol:
          return (Ls,Us)
       sqrt=np.sqrt(disc)
       r1=(-b - sqrt)/(2*a); r2=(-b + sqrt)/(2*a)
       if r1 > r2: swap
       segs=[(-np.inf, r1), (r2, np.inf)]
    newL=[];newU=[]
    for segL,segU in segs:
       for L,U in zip(Ls,Us):
          l=L
          if segL > l: l=segL
          u=U
          if segU < u: u=segU
          if l <= u + tol:
             # also clamp to existing domain? Already within Ls/Us which within [lo,hi]
             newL.append(l); newU.append(u)
    if not newL: return None
    # merge
    pairs=sorted(zip(newL,newU))
    mL=[pairs[0][0]]; mU=[pairs[0][1]]
    for L,U in pairs[1:]:
       if L <= mU[-1] + tol:
          if U > mU[-1]: mU[-1]=U
       else:
          mL.append(L); mU.append(U)
    if mU[0] < last? Loop ok.
    return (mL,mU)
```
For concave disc<0 all feasible. Is that always? p=ax^2+bx+c, a<0, disc<0 => no real roots and sign = a<0 all x, so p<0 all, yes.
For convex disc<0 => p>0 all (since a>0) no feasible.
For a>0, if disc=0, feasible at one root; intersection may be point. Our disc<0 only negative, set 0. Good.

`feasible_intervals(...)` top-level:
```
def feasible_intervals(lo, hi, const_max, pos_a1, pos_a0, neg_a1, neg_a0, quad_a2, quad_a1, quad_a0, t, tol=1e-12):
    if t < const_max - tol: return None
    L=lo; U=hi
    npos=len(pos_a1)
    if npos:
       vals=(t - pos_a0)/pos_a1
       umin=float(np.min(vals))
       if umin < U: U=umin
    nneg=len(neg_a1)
    if nneg:
       vals=(t - neg_a0)/neg_a1
       lmax=float(np.max(vals))
       if lmax > L: L=lmax
    if L > U + tol: return None
    Ls=[L]; Us=[U]
    for idx in range(len(quad_a2)):
       res=intersect_quad_intervals(Ls,Us, float(quad_a2[idx]), float(quad_a1[idx]), float(quad_a0[idx]), t, tol)
       if res is None: return None
       Ls,Us=res
    return (Ls,Us)
```
Need ensure if L<lo? We initialize lo,hi. Constraints may produce ub<lo -> L>U false? If ub<lo, U becomes <L, false. If lb>hi, L>U false. Good. But line division by negative neg: if pos/neg arrays empty.

Potential issue: line constraints with pos_a1 maybe extremely small causing huge ub/inf. But we classify |a1|>eps. Good.

Classification:
```
q_a2 = A2[active_idx]; q_a1=A1[active_idx]; q_a0=A0[active_idx]
eps=1e-13
quad_mask = np.abs(q_a2) > eps
line_mask = ~quad_mask & (np.abs(q_a1) > eps)
const_mask = ~quad_mask & ~line_mask
const_max=unaff
if any const: const_max=max(const_max, float(np.max(q_a0[const_mask])))
pos_mask=line_mask & (q_a1>0)

`pair_coord_descent`:
Need manage best c5 using local compute. Since c unscaled, c5=cmax*2/n.
Function signature:
```
def pair_coord_descent(h, n, max_sweeps, deadline, rng, reserve=20):
    h = quick_project(h,n)
    c = correlate_array(h)
    best_val = c.max()
    best_h = h.copy()
    struct = build_structures(n)
    stop = deadline - reserve
    sweep=0
    while sweep < max_sweeps and time.time() < stop:
       # maybe if sweep >= some, use random patterns more
       if sweep % 5 == 0:
          pattern=0
       elif sweep %5==1: pattern=1
       elif sweep%5==2: pattern=2
       elif sweep%5==3: pattern=3
       else: pattern=4
       for u in range(n):
          if time.time() >= stop: break
          if pattern==0:
             v=(u+1)%n
          elif pattern==1:
             v=(u-1+n)%n
          elif pattern==2:
             v=(u + max(1,n//2))%n
          elif pattern==3:
             v=(u*7 + 13)%n
             if v==u: v=(v+1)%n
          else:
             # random distinct
             # To avoid O(1) random with possible same, pick offset
             v=(u + int(1 + rng.randint(0, n-1)))%n
             if v==u: v=(u+1)%n
          h,c,improved = pair_update(h,c,u,v,struct,iters=32)
       # after sweep
       c = correlate_array(h)
       h = quick_project(h,n)
       cmax = float(c.max())
       if cmax < best_val:
          best_val=cmax; best_h=h.copy()
       sweep += 1
    return best_h, float(best_val)*2.0/n
```
Potential issue: pair_update modifies h and c, then after sweep recompute. But if pair_update skipped due no improvement, c remains. Good.
`max_sweeps` could be large; loop until deadline. Use pattern random based on sweep.
Time inside pair_update not checked. If an update >10s? No.

But pair_update binary calls feasible_intervals 30 times, each with quadratics. For some updates, const_max feasible (t=unaff) perhaps quickly? We can first check at const_max; if feasible, binary skipped, saving. Good.

else:

     mid=(low+high)*0.5

```

### Multi-resolution interpolation and feasibility

```
def interpolate_h(h_src,n_src,n_dst):
    xp=(np.arange(n_src)+0.5)*(2.0/n_src)
    xn=(np.arange(n_dst)+0.5)*(2.0/n_dst)
    h_new=np.interp(xn,xp,h_src)
    return project_feasible(h_new,n_dst)
```
But if n_dst smaller, interpolation may smooth. OK.

`make_gaussian_perturbation(h,n,rng,scale)`:

def make_gaussian_perturbation(h,n,rng,scale):
    p = h + scale * rng.randn(n)
    return project_fast(p,n)
```
`make_binary_perturbation`:
```
def make_binary_perturbation(h,n,rng,scale=0.05):
    if n % 2 != 0:
       p = h + scale*rng.randn(n)
       return project_fast(p,n)
    p = h + scale*rng.randn(n)
    target = n//2
    b = (p >= 0.5).astype(float)
    sumb = int(np.sum(b))
    if sumb > target:
       idx1 = np.nonzero(b > 0.5)[0]
       order = idx1[np.argsort(p[idx1])]  # ascending p
       excess = sumb - target
       for k in range(min(excess,len(order))): b[order[k]]=0.0
    elif sumb < target:
       idx0 = np.nonzero(b < 0.5)[0]
       order = idx0[np.argsort(-p[idx0])]
       deficit = target - sumb
       for k in range(min(deficit,len(order))): b[order[k]]=1.0
    return b
```
No lambdas.

`project_fast` for binary exact: binary sum exact int, target int, so ok; but due clip maybe. Project handles.

### Detailed code for pair_update

Let's write carefully.

```
def pair_update(h, c, u, v, struct, iters=32, tol=1e-10):
    if u == v:
        return h, c, False
    first_m, first_b, second_m, second_a, masks, n, M, center = struct
    ou = float(h[u]); ov=float(h[v]); s=ou+ov
    lo = s - 1.0
    if lo < 0.0: lo = 0.0
    hi = s
    if hi > 1.0: hi = 1.0
    if hi - lo <= 1e-14:
        return h,c,False
    A0 = c.copy()
    A1 = np.zeros(M, dtype=float)
    A2 = np.zeros(M, dtype=float)
    # self
    oldc = ou*(1.0-ou) + ov*(1.0-ov)
    A2[center] += -2.0
    A1[center] += 2.0*s
    A0[center] += (s - s*s) - oldc
    # cross
    du = v - u
    m1 = center + du
    m2 = center - du
    if 0 <= m1 < M:
       A2[m1] += 1.0
       A1[m1] += 1.0 - s
       A0[m1] -= ou*(1.0 - ov)
    if 0 <= m2 < M and m2 != m1:
       A2[m2] += 1.0
       A1[m2] += -(1.0 + s)
       A0[m2] += s - ov*(1.0 - ou)
```
Need consider if m1 or m2 equals center? If u != v, du !=0, so m1,m2 != center? center±du, du could 0 no; ok. But if du maybe 0? skip.
But what if m1==m2? no.
However, if m1 or m2 equals center? no. If n small and m1 out? du ∈ [-(n-1),n-1], center+du in [0,M-1]. OK.
Linear:
```
    mu_first_m = first_m[u]
    bu = first_b[u]
    masku = (bu != u) & (bu != v)
    if np.any(masku):
        mm = mu_first_m[masku]
        hb = h[bu[masku]]
        A1[mm] += 1.0 - hb
        A0[mm] += -ou * (1.0 - hb)
    mu_second_m = second_m[u]
    au = second_a[u]
    masku2 = (au != u) & (au != v)
    if np.any(masku2):
        mm = mu_second_m[masku2]
        ha = h[au[masku2]]
        A1[mm] -= ha
        A0[mm] += ou * ha
    mv_first_m = first_m[v]
    bv = first_b[v]
    maskv = (bv != u) & (bv != v)
    if np.any(maskv):
        mm = mv_first_m[maskv]
        hb = h[bv[maskv]]
        A1[mm] += -(1.0 - hb)
        A0[mm] += ou * (1.0 - hb)
    mv_second_m = second_m[v]
    av = second_a[v]
    maskv2 = (av != u) & (av != v)
    if np.any(maskv2):
        mm = mv_second_m[maskv2]
        ha = h[av[maskv2]]
        A1[mm] += ha
        A0[mm] += -ou * ha
```

```

    active_idx = np.nonzero(changed)[0]
    if active_idx.size == 0:
        return h,c,False
    not_changed = ~changed
    if np.any(not_changed):
        unaff = float(np.max(c[not_changed]))
    else:

Candidate eval:
```
    best_x = ou
    best_val = current_max
    # helper add candidate manually in loop
    cand_vals = [lo, hi, ou]
    for idx in range(len(Ls)):
       L=Ls[idx]; U=Us[idx]
       cand_vals.append(L); cand_vals.append(U); cand_vals.append(0.5*(L+U))
       if ou < L: cand_vals.append(L)
       elif ou > U: cand_vals.append(U)
       else: cand_vals.append(ou)
    for x in cand_vals:
       if x < lo - 1e-12: x = lo
       elif x > hi + 1e-12: x = hi
       if x < 0.0: x=0.0
       if x > 1.0: x=1.0
       if x < lo - tol or x > hi + tol: continue
       xclip = x
       val = unaff
       if qa2.size:
          vv = qa2*xclip*xclip + qa1*xclip + qa0
          mval = float(np.max(vv))
          if mval > val: val = mval
       # if val close and x closer old, update
       if val < best_val - tol or (abs(val - best_val) <= tol and abs(xclip - ou) < abs(best_x - ou)):
           best_val = val; best_x = xclip
    if best_val < current_max - tol:
       xc = best_x
       if xc < lo: xc=lo
       if xc > hi: xc=hi
       h[u]=xc
       h[v]=s-xc
       # numerical bounds
       if h[u] < 0.0 and h[u] > -1e-12: h[u]=0.0
       if h[u] > 1.0 and h[u] < 1.0+1e-12: h[u]=1.0
       if h[v] < 0.0 and h[v] > -1e-12: h[v]=0.0
       if h[v] > 1.0 and h[v] < 1.0+1e-12: h[v]=1.0
       if active_idx.size:
          new_vals = qa2*xc*xc + qa1*xc + qa0
          c[active_idx] = new_vals
          # if clipping changed xc? h values changed but c based on xc. Should use actual h? We set h from xc; ok.
       improved = True
       return h,c,improved
    return h,c,False

### Pair_update and c.max() after update
We don't recompute c.max; pair_coord tracks via recompute each sweep. Within sweep, c updated; current_max in subsequent pair_update = float(np.max(c)) O(M). Good.

### Pair_coord_descent use of best_h and c5
At start, `c5_val = c.max()*2/n`. After each update, if current c max lower, but final recompute. For best_h save only after sweep to avoid copying h each update. Could miss best if deadline hits inside sweep before recompute; at end of function we recompute current and save. Good.
Implementation:
```
def pair_coord_descent(h,n,max_sweeps,deadline,rng,reserve=10):
    h = quick_project(h,n)
    c = correlate_array(h)
    best_cmax = float(c.max()); best_h=h.copy()
    struct=build_structures(n)
    stop=deadline-reserve
    no_imp=0
    sweep=0
    while sweep < max_sweeps:
       if time.time() >= stop: break
       # perturbation if no imp
       if no_imp >= 5 and n>1:
          h = make_gaussian_perturbation(h,n,rng,0.02)
          c = correlate_array(h)
          no_imp=0
       pattern = sweep % 5
       improved_sweep=False
       for u in range(n):
          if time.time() >= stop: break
          if pattern==0: v=(u+1)%n
          elif pattern==1: v=(u+n-1)%n
          elif pattern==2: v=(u + max(1,n//2))%n
          elif pattern==3:
             v=(u*7 + 13) % n
             if v==u: v=(v+1)%n
          else:
             v=(u + int(1 + rng.randint(0, n-1)))%n
             if v==u: v=(u+1)%n
          h,c,imp=pair_update(h,c,u,v,struct,iters=32)
          if imp: improved_sweep=True
       c = correlate_array(h)
       h = quick_project(h,n)
       cmax = float(c.max())
       if cmax < best_cmax - 1e-12:
          best_cmax=cmax; best_h=h.copy(); improved_sweep=True
       if not improved_sweep:
          no_imp += 1
       else:
          no_imp = 0
       sweep += 1
    c = correlate_array(h)
    h = quick_project(h,n)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    if cmax < best_cmax: best_h=h.copy(); best_cmax=cmax
    return best_h, float(best_cmax)*(2.0/float(n))
```
`make_gaussian_perturbation` uses project_feasible, may be expensive if called often. Called only after 5 no-imp sweeps. OK.
Pattern 3 with n maybe not coprime? if v==u adjust.
Random pattern: `rng.randint(0, n-1)` returns 0..n-2; offset 1..n-1. Good.

Potential issue: In pair_coord_descent after perturbation, `improved_sweep` false maybe; if cmax > best, best kept. OK.

### Test select_n_candidates loops:
```
def select_n_candidates(best_n, max_n=512):
    base=[]
    for n2 in (64,96,128,160,192,224,256,288,320,360,400,448,512): base.append(n2)
    rel=[]
    for mult in (0.75,0.85,1.0,1.15,1.3,1.5,1.75,2.0):
       n2=int(round(best_n*mult))
       if n2%2: n2+=1
       rel.append(n2)
    cands=[]
    for n2 in base:
       if 48<=n2<=max_n and n2!=best_n and (n2 not in cands): cands.append(n2)
    for n2 in rel: ...
    cands.sort()
    selected=[]
    for n2 in reversed(cands):
       if n2 < best_n:
          selected.append(n2); break
    for n2 in cands:
       if n2 > best_n:
          selected.append(n2); break
    for n2 in cands:
       if len(selected) >= 4: break
       if n2 not in selected:
          selected.append(n2)
    final=[]
    for n2 in selected:
       if n2 != best_n and n2 not in final:
          final.append(n2)
    return final[:4]
```
This may select very low then high depending order. We added lower close, upper close, then from start (low) until 4. Good.

### In run multi-res allocation
```
selected = select_n_candidates(best_n)
remaining_end = work_end - 10
i=0
while i < len(selected) and time.time() < remaining_end - 80:
   n2=selected[i]
   time_left = remaining_end - time.time()
   alloc = min(240.0, time_left - 80.0)
   if alloc < 60: break
   h0=interpolate_h(best_h,best_n,n2)
   dl = time.time() + alloc
   h1,c1=pair_coord_descent(h0,n2,10000,dl,rng,reserve=5)
   if c1<best_c5: update
   i+=1
```
If n2 == best_n? select excludes. Could re-optimize best_n after perturb? Not necessary.

### Perturb attempts allocation
```
styles = [( 'g', 0.01), ('g',0.02), ('g',0.05), ('g',0.1), ('b',0.05), ('g',0.2)]

```python
import time
import numpy as np

def correlate_internal(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    # Work with the reversed full-correlation order used by the update formulas.
    return corr[::-1].copy()

def compute_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    corr = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(corr)) * dx

def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size != n:
        tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        m = min(n, h.size)
        if m > 0:
            tmp[:m] = h[:m]
        h = tmp
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)

    for _ in range(80):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = np.argsort(1.0 - h)[::-1]
            rem = diff
            for ii in idx:
                room = 1.0 - h[ii]
                if room <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                add = room if room < rem else rem
                if add <= 0.0:
                    continue
                h[ii] += add
                rem -= add
                if rem <= 1e-15:
                    break
        else:
            idx = np.argsort(h)[::-1]
            rem = -diff
            for ii in idx:
                avail = h[ii]
                if avail <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                add = avail if avail < rem else rem
                if add <= 0.0:
                    continue
                h[ii] -= add
                rem -= add
                if rem <= 1e-15:
                    break

    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if diff > 1e-14:
        ii = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
        add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[ii])
        if add > 0.0:
            h[ii] += add
    elif diff < -1e-14:
        ii = int(np.argmin(h))
        add = max(diff, -h[ii])
        if add < 0.0:
            h[ii] += add
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    return h

def quick_project(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    for _ in range(12):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-14
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt == 0:
                break
            h[mask] += diff / float(cnt)
        else:
            mask = h > 1e-14
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if cnt == 0:
                break
            h[mask] += diff / float(cnt)
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)

    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if diff > 1e-14:
        ii = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
        add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[ii])
        if add > 0.0:
            h[ii] += add
    elif diff < -1e-14:
        ii = int(np.argmin(h))
        add = max(diff, -h[ii])
        if add < 0.0:
            h[ii] += add
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    return h

def build_structures(n):
    m_total = 2 * n - 1
    center = n - 1
    first_m = [None] * n
    first_b = [None] * n
    second_m = [None] * n
    second_a = [None] * n
    masks = [None] * n

    for i in range(n):
        d_first = np.arange(-i, n - i, dtype=np.int64)
        m_first = center + d_first
        b_first = i + d_first

        d_second = np.arange(i - n + 1, i + 1, dtype=np.int64)
        m_second = center + d_second
        a_second = i - d_second

        mask = np.zeros(m_total, dtype=bool)
        mask[m_first] = True
        mask[m_second] = True

        first_m[i] = m_first
        first_b[i] = b_first
        second_m[i] = m_second
        second_a[i] = a_second
        masks[i] = mask

    return first_m, first_b, second_m, second_a, masks, n, m_total, center

def intersect_quad_intervals(Ls, Us, a, b, c0, t, tol=1e-10):
    if abs(a) <= 1e-14:
        if abs(b) <= 1e-14:
            if c0 - t > tol:
                return None
            return Ls, Us

        newL = []
        newU = []
        if b > 0.0:
            ub = (t - c0) / b
            for L, U in zip(Ls, Us):
                if U > ub:
                    U = ub
                if L <= U + tol:
                    newL.append(L)
                    newU.append(U)
        else:
            lb = (t - c0) / b
            for L, U in zip(Ls, Us):
                if L < lb:
                    L = lb
                if L <= U + tol:
                    newL.append(L)
                    newU.append(U)

        if not newL:
            return None
        return newL, newU

    c = c0 - t
    disc = b * b - 4.0 * a * c

    if a > 0.0:
        if disc < -tol:
            return None
        if disc < 0.0:
            disc = 0.0
        sq = np.sqrt(disc)
        r1 = (-b - sq) / (2.0 * a)
        r2 = (-b + sq) / (2.0 * a)
        if r1 > r2:
            r1, r2 = r2, r1
        segs = [(r1, r2)]
    else:
        if disc < -tol:
            return Ls, Us
        if disc <= tol:
            return Ls, Us
        sq = np.sqrt(disc)
        r1 = (-b - sq) / (2.0 * a)
        r2 = (-b + sq) / (2.0 * a)
        if r1 > r2:
            r1, r2 = r2, r1
        segs = [(-np.inf, r1), (r2, np.inf)]

    newL = []
    newU = []
    for segL, segU in segs:
        for L, U in zip(Ls, Us):
            l = L if L > segL else segL
            u = U if U < segU else segU
            if l <= u + tol:
                newL.append(l)
                newU.append(u)

    if not newL:
        return None

    pairs = sorted(zip(newL, newU))
    mL = [pairs[0][0]]
    mU = [pairs[0][1]]
    for L, U in pairs[1:]:
        if L <= mU[-1] + tol:
            if U > mU[-1]:
                mU[-1] = U
        else:
            mL.append(L)
            mU.append(U)
    return mL, mU

def feasible_intervals(lo, hi, const_max, pos_a1, pos_a0, neg_a1, neg_a0,
                       quad_a2, quad_a1, quad_a0, t, tol=1e-10):
    if t < const_max - tol:
        return None

    L = lo
    U = hi

    if len(pos_a1) > 0:
        cand = (t - pos_a0) / pos_a1
        umin = float(np.min(cand))
        if umin < U:
            U = umin

    if len(neg_a1) > 0:
        cand = (t - neg_a0) / neg_a1
        lmax = float(np.max(cand))
        if lmax > L:
            L = lmax

    if L > U + tol:
        return None

    Ls = [L]
    Us = [U]

    if len(quad_a2) == 0:
        return Ls, Us

    for i in range(len(quad_a2)):
        res = intersect_quad_intervals(
            Ls, Us,
            float(quad_a2[i]), float(quad_a1[i]), float(quad_a0[i]),
            t, tol
        )
        if res is None:
            return None
        Ls, Us = res

    return Ls, Us

def pair_update(h, c, u, v, struct, iters=32, tol=1e-10):
    if u == v:
        return h, c, False

    first_m, first_b, second_m, second_a, masks, n, m_total, center = struct

    ou = float(h[u])
    ov = float(h[v])
    s = ou + ov

    lo = s - 1.0
    if lo < 0.0:
        lo = 0.0
    hi = s
    if hi > 1.0:
        hi = 1.0
    if hi - lo <= 1e-14:
        return h, c, False

    A0 = c.copy()
    A1 = np.zeros(m_total, dtype=float)
    A2 = np.zeros(m_total, dtype=float)

    old_center = ou * (1.0 - ou) + ov * (1.0 - ov)
    A2[center] += -2.0
    A1[center] += 2.0 * s
    A0[center] += (s - s * s) - old_center

    du = v - u
    m1 = center + du
    m2 = center - du

    if 0 <= m1 < m_total:
        A2[m1] += 1.0
        A1[m1] += 1.0 - s
        A0[m1] -= ou * (1.0 - ov)

    if 0 <= m2 < m_total and m2 != m1:
        A2[m2] += 1.0
        A1[m2] += -(1.0 + s)
        A0[m2] += s - ov * (1.0 - ou)

    mm = first_m[u]
    bb = first_b[u]
    mask = (bb != u) & (bb != v)
    if np.any(mask):
        idx = mm[mask]
        hb = h[bb[mask]]
        A1[idx] += 1.0 - hb
        A0[idx] += -ou * (1.0 - hb)

    mm = second_m[u]
    aa = second_a[u]
    mask = (aa != u) & (aa != v)
    if np.any(mask):
        idx = mm[mask]
        ha = h[aa[mask]]
        A1[idx] -= ha
        A0[idx] += ou * ha

    mm = first_m[v]
    bb = first_b[v]
    mask = (bb != u) & (bb != v)
    if np.any(mask):
        idx = mm[mask]
        hb = h[bb[mask]]
        A1[idx] += -(1.0 - hb)
        A0[idx] += ou * (1.0 - hb)

    mm = second_m[v]
    aa = second_a[v]
    mask = (aa != u) & (aa != v)
    if np.any(mask):
        idx = mm[mask]
        ha = h[aa[mask]]
        A1[idx] += ha
        A0[idx] += -ou * ha

    eps = 1e-13
    changed = (np.abs(A2) > eps) | (np.abs(A1) > eps) | (np.abs(A0 - c) > 1e-11)
    active_idx = np.nonzero(changed)[0]
    if active_idx.size == 0:
        return h, c, False

    not_changed = ~changed
    if np.any(not_changed):
        unaff = float(np.max(c[not_changed]))
    else:
        unaff = 0.0

    qa2 = A2[active_idx]
    qa1 = A1[active_idx]
    qa0 = A0[active_idx]

    const_max = unaff
    zero_quad = np.abs(qa2) <= eps
    line_large = zero_quad & (np.abs(qa1) > eps)
    const_mask = zero_quad & ~line_large

    if np.any(const_mask):
        cm = float(np.max(qa0[const_mask]))
        if cm > const_max:
            const_max = cm

    pos_mask = line_large & (qa1 > 0.0)
    neg_mask = line_large & (qa1 < 0.0)

    pos_a1 = qa1[pos_mask]
    pos_a0 = qa0[pos_mask]
    neg_a1 = qa1[neg_mask]
    neg_a0 = qa0[neg_mask]

    quad_mask = ~zero_quad
    quad_a2 = qa2[quad_mask]
    quad_a1 = qa1[quad_mask]
    quad_a0 = qa0[quad_mask]

    current_max = float(np.max(c))
    if const_max > current_max:
        low = 0.0
    else:
        low = const_max
    high = current_max
    if high < low:
        high = current_max + 1.0

    res_low = feasible_intervals(
        lo, hi, const_max, pos_a1, pos_a0, neg_a1, neg_a0,
        quad_a2, quad_a1, quad_a0, low, tol
    )
    if res_low is not None:
        t = low
    else:
        for _ in range(iters):
            mid = 0.5 * (low + high)
            res_mid = feasible_intervals(
                lo, hi, const_max, pos_a1, pos_a0, neg_a1, neg_a0,
                quad_a2, quad_a1, quad_a0, mid, tol
            )
            if res_mid is not None:
                high = mid
            else:
                low = mid
        t = high

    res = feasible_intervals(
        lo, hi, const_max, pos_a1, pos_a0, neg_a1, neg_a0,
        quad_a2, quad_a1, quad_a0, t, tol
    )
    if res is None:
        res = feasible_intervals(
            lo, hi, const_max, pos_a1, pos_a0, neg_a1, neg_a0,
            quad_a2, quad_a1, quad_a0, current_max, tol
        )
        if res is None:
            return h, c, False
        t = current_max

    Ls, Us = res

    best_x = ou
    best_val = current_max

    candidates = [lo, hi, ou]
    for i in range(len(Ls)):
        L = Ls[i]
        U = Us[i]
        candidates.append(L)
        candidates.append(U)
        candidates.append(0.5 * (L + U))
        if ou < L:
            candidates.append(L)
        elif ou > U:
            candidates.append(U)
        else:
            candidates.append(ou)

    for x in candidates:
        if x < lo - tol:
            continue
        if x > hi + tol:
            continue
        xc = x
        if xc < lo:
            xc = lo
        if xc > hi:
            xc = hi
        if xc < 0.0:
            xc = 0.0
        if xc > 1.0:
            xc = 1.0

        val = unaff
        if qa2.size > 0:
            vv = qa2 * xc * xc + qa1 * xc + qa0
            mv = float(np.max(vv))
            if mv > val:
                val = mv

        if val < best_val - tol:
            best_val = val
            best_x = xc
        elif abs(val - best_val) <= tol and abs(xc - ou) < abs(best_x - ou):
            best_val = val
            best_x = xc

    if best_val < current_max - 1e-13:
        xc = best_x
        if xc < lo:
            xc = lo
        if xc > hi:
            xc = hi
        if xc < 0.0:
            xc = 0.0
        if xc > 1.0:
            xc = 1.0

        h[u] = xc
        h[v] = s - xc

        if h[u] < 0.0 and h[u] > -1e-12:
            h[u] = 0.0
        if h[u] > 1.0 and h[u] < 1.0 + 1e-12:
            h[u] = 1.0
        if h[v] < 0.0 and h[v] > -1e-12:
            h[v] = 0.0
        if h[v] > 1.0 and h[v] < 1.0 + 1e-12:
            h[v] = 1.0

        if active_idx.size > 0:
            new_vals = qa2 * xc * xc + qa1 * xc + qa0
            c[active_idx] = new_vals

        return h, c, True

    return h, c, False

def pair_coord_descent(h, n, max_sweeps, deadline, rng, reserve=10.0):
    h = quick_project(h, n)
    c = correlate_internal(h)
    best_cmax = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy()

    struct = build_structures(n)
    stop = deadline - reserve
    no_improve = 0
    sweep = 0

    while sweep < max_sweeps:
        if time.time() >= stop:
            break

        if no_improve >= 5:
            h = make_gaussian_perturbation(h, n, rng, 0.02)
            c = correlate_internal(h)
            no_improve = 0

        pattern = sweep % 5
        improved_sweep = False

        for u in range(n):
            if time.time() >= stop:
                break

            if pattern == 0:
                v = (u + 1) % n
            elif pattern == 1:
                v = (u - 1) % n
            elif pattern == 2:
                v = (u + max(1, n // 2)) % n
            elif pattern == 3:
                v = (u * 7 + 13) % n
                if v == u:
                    v = (v + 1) % n
            else:
                v = (u + int(1 + rng.randint(0, n - 1))) % n
                if v == u:
                    v = (u + 1) % n

            h, c, improved = pair_update(h, c, u, v, struct, iters=32)
            if improved:
                improved_sweep = True

        h = quick_project(h, n)
        c = correlate_internal(h)
        cmax = float(c.max())

        if cmax < best_cmax - 1e-12:
            best_cmax = cmax
            best_h = h.copy()
            improved_sweep = True

        if improved_sweep:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        sweep += 1

    h = quick_project(h, n)
    c = correlate_internal(h)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax < best_cmax:
        best_cmax = cmax
        best_h = h.copy()

    return best_h, float(best_cmax) * (2.0 / float(n))

def interpolate_h(h_src, n_src, n_dst):
    if n_src == n_dst:
        return quick_project(h_src, n_dst)
    xs = (np.arange(n_src) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n_src))
    xd = (np.arange(n_dst) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n_dst))
    h_new = np.interp(xd, xs, h_src)
    return quick_project(h_new, n_dst)

def make_gaussian_perturbation(h, n, rng, scale):
    p = h + scale * rng.randn(n)
    return project_feasible(p, n)

def make_swap_perturbation(h, n, rng, swaps=30):
    p = h.copy()
    for _ in range(swaps):
        i = int(rng.randint(0, n))
        j = int(rng.randint(0, n))
        a = p[i]
        p[i] = p[j]
        p[j] = a
    return quick_project(p, n)

def make_binary_perturbation(h, n, rng, scale=0.05):
    if n <= 2:
        return make_gaussian_perturbation(h, n, rng, max(0.01, scale))

    p = h + scale * rng.randn(n)
    b = (p >= 0.5).astype(float)
    target = int(round(0.5 * float(n)))

    cnt = int(np.sum(b))
    if cnt > target:
        idx1 = np.nonzero(b > 0.5)[0]
        order = idx1[np.argsort(p[idx1])]
        excess = cnt - target
        stop = min(excess, len(order))
        for k in range(stop):
            b[order[k]] = 0.0
    elif cnt < target:
        idx0 = np.nonzero(b < 0.5)[0]
        order = idx0[np.argsort(-p[idx0])]
        deficit = target - cnt
        stop = min(deficit, len(order))
        for k in range(stop):
            b[order[k]] = 1.0

    return project_feasible(b, n)

def select_n_candidates(best_n, max_n=512):
    base = [64, 96, 128, 160, 192, 224, 256, 288, 320, 360, 400, 448, 512]
    rel = []
    for mult in (0.75, 0.85, 1.0, 1.15, 1.3, 1.5, 1.75, 2.0):
        n2 = int(round(best_n * mult))
        if n2 % 2:
            n2 += 1
        rel.append(n2)

    cands = []
    for n2 in base:
        if 48 <= n2 <= max_n and n2 != best_n and n2 not in cands:
            cands.append(n2)
    for n2 in rel:
        if 48 <= n2 <= max_n and n2 != best_n and n2 not in cands:
            cands.append(n2)

    cands.sort()

    lower = []
    upper = []
    for n2 in cands:
        if n2 < best_n:
            lower.append(n2)
        elif n2 > best_n:
            upper.append(n2)

    selected = []
    if len(lower) > 0:
        selected.append(lower[-1])
    if len(upper) > 0:
        selected.append(upper[0])
    if len(upper) > 2:
        selected.append(upper[len(upper) // 2])
    if len(upper) > 0:
        selected.append(upper[-1])
    if len(lower) > 0:
        selected.append(lower[0])

    final = []
    for n2 in selected:
        if n2 != best_n and n2 not in final:
            final.append(n2)
    return final[:4]

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    total = float(budget_s)
    reserve = 10.0
    if total <= reserve + 5.0:
        reserve = min(2.0, max(0.5, total * 0.1))
    work_end = t0 + total - reserve
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    best_h = None
    best_c5 = float("inf")
    best_n = 0

    try:
        ph = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        if ph.ndim == 1 and ph.size >= 10:
            pn = int(ph.size)
            h0 = project_feasible(ph, pn)
            c0 = compute_c5(h0)
            best_h = h0
            best_c5 = float(c0)
            best_n = pn
    except Exception:
        best_h = None

    if best_h is None or best_n <= 0:
        best_n = 192
        t = np.arange(best_n)
        h0 = 0.5 + 0.35 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t / float(best_n))
        best_h = project_feasible(h0, best_n)
        best_c5 = compute_c5(best_h)

    if total > 60.0:
        if total > 250.0:
            main_deadline = t0 + 0.55 * (total - reserve)
        else:
            main_deadline = work_end
        if main_deadline > work_end:
            main_deadline = work_end

        h_try, c_try = pair_coord_descent(
            best_h, best_n, 100000, main_deadline, rng, reserve=5.0
        )
        if c_try < best_c5 - 1e-15:
            best_c5 = float(c_try)
            best_h = h_try
            best_n = int(best_n)

    if total > 180.0 and time.time() < work_end - 90.0:
        selected = select_n_candidates(best_n, max_n=512)
        for n_try in selected:
            if time.time() > work_end - 80.0:
                break
            remain = work_end - time.time()
            alloc = min(240.0, remain - 80.0)
            if alloc < 40.0:
                break
            h_init = interpolate_h(best_h, best_n, n_try)
            c_init = compute_c5(h_init)
            if c_init < best_c5 - 1e-15:
                best_c5 = float(c_init)
                best_h = h_init
                best_n = int(n_try)

            dl = time.time() + alloc
            h_try, c_try = pair_coord_descent(
                h_init, n_try, 100000, dl, rng, reserve=5.0
            )
            if c_try < best_c5 - 1e-15:
                best_c5 = float(c_try)
                best_h = h_try
                best_n = int(n_try)

    if total > 220.0 and time.time() < work_end - 70.0:
        perturb_attempts = [
            ("g", 0.01),
            ("s", 0.0),
            ("g", 0.02),
            ("g", 0.05),
            ("b", 0.05),
            ("g", 0.10),
            ("s", 0.0),
            ("g", 0.20),
        ]
        for style, scale in perturb_attempts:
            if time.time() > work_end - 60.0:
                break
            remain = work_end - time.time()
            alloc = min(180.0, remain - 40.0)
            if alloc < 30.0:
                break

            if style == "g":
                h0 = make_gaussian_perturbation(best_h, best_n, rng, scale)
            elif style == "s":
                h0 = make_swap_perturbation(best_h, best_n, rng, swaps=40)
            else:
                h0 = make_binary_perturbation(best_h, best_n, rng, scale)

            h0 = quick_project(h0, best_n)
            c0 = compute_c5(h0)
            if c0 < best_c5 - 1e-15:
                best_c5 = float(c0)
                best_h = h0

            dl = time.time() + alloc
            h_try, c_try = pair_coord_descent(
                h0, best_n, 100000, dl, rng, reserve=5.0
            )
            if c_try < best_c5 - 1e-15:
                best_c5 = float(c_try)
                best_h = h_try
                best_n = int(best_n)

    if time.time() < work_end - 25.0:
        h_try, c_try = pair_coord_descent(
            best_h, best_n, 100000, work_end - 2.0, rng, reserve=4.0
        )
        if c_try < best_c5 - 1e-15:
            best_c5 = float(c_try)
            best_h = h_try
            best_n = int(best_n)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    final_c5 = compute_c5(best_h)

    return (best_h, float(final_c5), int(best_n))
```
